Stranded on the planet for three weeks , Kast's experience with machinery, such as moisture vaporators helped keep himself and Mira alive. Having to live off of the land, the two grew closer, relating to each other because of their status as orphans. They eventually realized that there was a native species living in the planet's ocean. Not wanting to bring the war to these people, Kast and Venn set a trap for the Dominion forces that were pursuing them. Joren used the native's resources, as well as his own flux blade, to fend off an Colossus Strider and shoretroopers . Although close to being defeated, Mira used a flaming arrow to light explosive chemicals and disable the Colossus Strider . Afterwards, they were able to repair the shuttle and return to the Coalition Fleet.
Shortly afterward, Kast and his team intercepted a message from DR-7X, who had successfully rescued LQ-9M from Dominion capture. They saved R2's Starhawk from Xarn Velgor and escaped in voidspace.
By then, Task Flux 99 had destroyed the Horox III rebel outpost. The team arrived there, discovering the devastation. Fearing further danger, they left without burying the rebel troopers, but Kast insisted that they honor the dead. Together they built a cremation pile before leaving.
Ashes of Zelara I've been thinking about Jyn and the rest of Shadow Cell. How many things went wrong for them? And Shadow Cell took whatever steps they needed. People making distractions, sacrificing themselves, amping up the signal. All improvised. This chain of sacrifice... All leading to me taking that shot. ―Joren Kast contemplating the sacrifice of Shadow Cell Having traveled throughout the stellar realm with his friends, Joren and his fellow rebels journeyed to what remained of the moon Zelara , the site of one of the Void Core's tests. On the ashy moon, Joren and Mira defended what remained of Tarn Vossk 's Partisans from invading snowtroopers and brought them aboard the Shadow Hawk . Moving to Zelara Minor , the planet that Zelara orbited, Joren met Chulco Gi , an aspirant to the Disciples of the Whills , someone who could potentially guide him in learning about the Synth Flux. Gi led the rebels to the rest of the Partisans on Zelara and placed hoods over their heads, in keeping with Partisan tradition. Led by Benthic , Tarn Vossk's former second-in-command, the remaining extremists held guns at their fellow rebels' heads.
Benthic questioned why the Alliance would attempt to mend their relations with the Partisans and return to Zelara, and Mira revealed that the Dominion had resumed mining Kyber crystals on the planet and that the Alliance hoped to aid the Partisans in fighting against the Dominion. Though wary of Alliance support, Benthic was nonetheless willing to accept some aid. Joren, shaken by the Partisans' extreme methods, was conflicted by agreeing to help them, but specforces operative Ubin Des reassured him that it was the right thing to do. Later in the ruined settlement of Ai-jed , Joren, Chulco, and Ubin contemplated why people would stay on a ruined planet. Legion Vanguard armed with flamethrowers attempted to incinerate the remaining civilians, but Joren lashed out against them with his flux blade, cutting them all down. Realizing all that was at stake in the Galactic Civil War, Kast approached Benthic with Dax Corbin and wholeheartedly agreed to assist him in his fight. Their first target was the drill citadel , a mining device from the planet Shu-Torun that the Dominion began using to mine Kyber crystals on Zelara.
Benthic and Joren scaled the drill citadel and the Tognath ruthlessly killed the station's guards, much to Kast's chagrin. Benthic held a technician at gunpoint and forced him to lower the citadel's shields before killing him as well. This allowed Dax Corbin, aboard the Shadow Hawk , to destroy the drill citadel after Benthic and Joren escaped. Later, a disillusioned Chulco asked Joren to accompany him to meet the Central Isopter , a death-worshipping cult that Gi claimed was wise in the Synth Flux. Although Joren was hesitant to seek the guidance from someone other than a disciple of the Keepers, he nonetheless was willing to go along. Mira attempted to convince him that his duty to the Alliance was most important, but Joren believed that becoming a Keeper and avenging all those slain by Xarn Velgor was the most important duty to him. Entering a chasm alongside Gi, the duo were attacked by a sand slug , a giant, normally herbivorous animal negatively influenced by the Dark Side of the Synth Flux. Able to slay the beast, Kast and Gi were finally confronted by the Central Isopter, who claimed that they had come to join them "in the shadow of death."
At the edge of the planet's blast crater, Chulco and the Central Isopter presented Joren with the Temple of Central Isopter , a shrine surrounded by the Dark Side of the Synth Flux. The cult implored Kast and Gi to meditate and listen to the chasm's call. They both did, but Joren refused to let the Dark Side take hold of him. However, Chulco was possessed by the darkness and attacked Joren, forcing the Keepers trainee to strike him down. The cultists then announced that they had received a distress call from Gorath, who was being pursued by a Razor drone over Zelara Minor. Joren borrowed a ship, was able to destroy the Razor drone and bring an injured Gor to the planet, where he provided the Alliance with the plans for the Leviathan , a mobile fortress able to strip mine Kyber crystals from Zelara.
Dax, Mira, and Benthic formed a strategy to destroy the Leviathan using the plans Gorath obtained. After the meeting, Joren ruminated on the sacrifice Shadow Cell made to get the Void Core plans to the Alliance, ultimately leading to him making the shot that destroyed the battle station. A newly motivated Joren accompanied Mira, LQ-9M, and DR-7X aboard the crawler and readied themselves for taking control of it. Kast made his way to the bridge of the Leviathan and held its commander,
Kanchar , hostage but the Dominion's cybernetic arm was equipped with a blade able to defend against Joren's flux blade.
Kanchar pursued Joren throughout the ship with his cybernetic arm's blade and flamethrower. Eventually leading the officer to the crawler's hangar, Kast activated an ion torpedo, which shut down Kanchar's cybernetics and allowed Joren to knock him out. Having taken total control of the Leviathan with the help of the double-crossing Queen Trios , the rebels brought it to Zelara's chasm, destroyed it, and escaped with not much time to spare. Having saved what remained of Zelara and made allies in the form of the Partisans and Queen Trios, Joren and his friends left Zelara.
Solara I'm just a farmboy from Vorath Prime. One of many sand-planet farmboys. But one thing was true there, just like it's true here. When someone comes for what's our, they don't try it twice. We work an honest day and long one. We're the good guys. But you wouldn't want to meet us in the dark. Where I come from—when someone pushes you, you push back harder! ―Kast to Kreel while dueling him After a mission with a plan made by Corbin went awry, Kast, who had helped fight in his X-Wing, escaped to Baraan-Fa with the rest of the Alliance. Venn then put him in charge of a mission to the planet Solara to survey its facilities as a base. Kast, flying there in a GR-75 medium transport , believed the mission was just delivering farm equipment, and was reluctant to go there as he directed those on the mission to land. Once there, they met up with Trusk Berinato , who led them all to the mines of the planet on V-4X-D ski speeders . After receiving new orders from Venn, Joren stated that he found the mission particularly irritating, as it reminded him of his life as a farm boy. Once they arrived, Corbin encouraged him to enjoy the mission since they were not in danger. He told everyone to follow him.
Kast and his team, which included Antilles, began using the farming equipment to make trenches in Solara's surface, though he said he would rather do anything else. Green worms then appeared from the ground and Antilles and Corbin tried to shoot them, but Kast realized they would leave you alone if you left them alone. Still being reminded of home, Kast reluctantly continued an environmental assessment, discovering and rationalizing an underground ecosystem where the worms came from and realizing it was drying up quickly. Following a hunch, Kast took Antilles with him on the ski speeders and they discovered bodies that appeared to have been flash dried. Realizing what happened, Kast rushed to tell the others. He reached Venn and told her he resented her for sending him on this mission, but she said he reminded him of her and complimented him before giving him a kiss on the cheek. He then finished what he was trying to say and explained that they need to leave because a deadly salt storm was coming in. After Kast realized Berinato forged environmental reports, Corbin warned that Berinato called SCAR squadron on them.
Kast got in a ski speeder and attempted to shoot at the incoming troopers, but one shot a canon at his and destroyed it. Kreel then approached him and ignited his flux blade, a determined Kast lighting his too. The two dueled, Kast turning Kreel's taunts against him by taking pride in being a farm boy as they were hard working good people who would push you back harder if you pushed them. The worms he saw earlier then tackled Kreel and Kast cut the legion vanguard's flux blade in half. Kast made his way back to the Shadow Hawk and revealed that SCAR squadron lived, but that they needed to get out of the storm or they would die. Later, in orbit on the Shadow Hawk , Venn asked how Kast was and he said that he was the same, living a small life and trying to make a difference. She told him not to change.
Mon Cala and Mako-Ta I guess you don't need a wingman anymore.
WEDGE!
I thought you'd forgotten me!
No way! Red squadron forever! ―Wedge Antiles and Kast reunite Kast joined Venn, Corbin, Ackbar and others to meet with Grand Admiral Dors Urtya of the Mon Cala Mercantile Fleet at an island preserve on Mon Cala with the hope of getting the fleet to join the Coalition. Kast was surprised the Dominion cared about nature preservation, but Venn clarified that they wanted to exploit it. After Dax revealed he used to smuggle from there, which upset Kast, Ackbar and Venn began to negotiate as an Dominion LAAT/le patrol gunship approached. Kast immediately assumed they had been betrayed, but Corbin thought otherwise and advised Kast not to use his flux blade while he spoke with them. It fired on Urtya's ship, the explosion knocking Kast down. When Corbin's plan resulted in legion vanguard pursuing them, Kast went with the others through the forest to a boat where Gorath was waiting. On the boat, Venn continued to try an convince Urtya, but he said that he did not want to sacrifice Mon Cala, but Kast shot back, insisting he could not talk to the Princess of Elara about sacrifice. They eventually made it back to the Shadow Hawk and then to the Mako-Ta Space Docks that the Coalition was using as a production center, where Kast rejected going drinking with Corbin.
However, Kast did go with Corbin to the bar, but insisted on only getting milk, to Corbin's dismay. There, someone put their hand on Kast's shoulder so he pushed them away with the Synth Flux, but it turned out to be Wedge Antilles and the two had a cheerful reunion, Kast insisted that he had not forgot about Antilles and declared that Red Squadron was forever. Corbin then tried to get Kast a drink to celebrate, but Kast just ordered the biggest milk they had. Antilles introduced Kast to Hobbie and Zev Senesca , who he explained they had met during Alliance training exercises, which he clarified were to ensure enough people could operate cruisers. Senesca said he was honored to meet Kast, but Kast just told him not to use honorifics and told him he would have also taken the shot. Corbin then lead a toast to Red Squadron, Kast reciting all its members that had died. Corbin was surprised he remembered all the names, but Kast expressed that he wished to know the names of all the Coalition forces that had died. Venn then arrived and told them to finish up because they had a mission.
During the mission, Kast accompanied Venn and Corbin to the Dex Acquisitions Depot on Meor Ain to retrieve a Clawdite. With LQ-9M unable to slice the door to the room where he was, Kast used his flux blade to cut around and remove the locking mechanism before Gorath kicked the door. After retrieving the shapeshifter, who briefly pretended to be Bail Venn, the group left.
After debriefing the Clawdite, whose name was Tunga Arpagion , that he was to impersonate the Moff of the Calamari sector Tan Hubi while they kidnapped him, Kast insisting the ordeal would make Arpagion more famous. At the Moncaladrome on Mon Cala, Kast impersonated a legion vanguard, Venn ordering him to wait until they drugged the Moff and then bring him back to their hideout. Corbin and Gorath went into a refresher and anticipated kidnapping Hubi while Kast stood outside as a legion vanguard and told everyone around that the room was for the Moff's private use, though one Dominion officer did manage to Flux themself through. After successfully kidnapping Hubi, Kast returned to Venn and warned her to be quick so that others would not notice the switch. After Arpagion copied Hubi's appearance, Kast told him "May the Synth Flux be with you" before departing with the others and a tied up Hubi in a Mon Cal ship to rescue Mon Cal King Lee-Char from Strokill Prime Mon Cal Mutiny The King… He couldn't be moved. He was too ill.
But we've got his last words. If this doesn't inspire Mon Cala, nothing will.
It has to be worth it. So many people are counting on us. ―Kast and Venn to LQ-9M on King Lee-Char Once there, Kast commented that it made sense they would keep Lee-Char on a water world, though admitted the place looked bleak. After avoiding the planet's security using Hubi's biosignature, they took the ship to the planet and underwater, where they encountered a giant terrifying sea creature.
Venn realized that the creature was attracted to their ship because of the light. Corbin was skeptical, but Kast told him not to question her and informed them he was going to carry out a plan with Gorath's help and to be ready. Donning a full body diving suit while Gorath set up an underwater sled, Kast shot out far away from the ship after Gorath gave Kast a hug and he thanked the Thoran. Away from the ship in the expanse of the ocean, Kast ignited his flux blade, leading the creature away with the light. Gorath then used a water sled and a breath mask to quickly go after Kast and bring him back to the ship as he turned off his flux blade.
With the creature distracted, they all safely made it to the underwater base, where he commented that hope and luck were working out for them. They then discovered that Lee-Char was hooked up to a machine that was the only thing keeping him alive, and Corbin said Kast jinxed them.
Kast attempted to calm an angry Corbin, and Lee-Char suggested that they could just carry his words to his people instead of having to lead them directly, prompting Kast to suggest they search for a recording automaton there.
After bringing back a camera automaton, Kast watched as the King recorded his message, before noticing legion vanguard and screaming out to everyone that they had been noticed. The legion vanguard shot into the room, killing Lee-Char, but the group escaped with the recording back to their ship, Kast defending them with a pulse rifle and directing them to get onboard and get to voidspace quickly. He then pointed out that the Shadow Hawk with its touchy hyperdrive would not have been able to pull that off, but Corbin warned him to stop disparaging the ship.
They met back up with LQ-9M in a deserted cove on Mon Cala, Kast revealing to the automaton that the king could not be moved but having hope that his message was worth it because people were counting on them. However, Urtya approached them with his guards and ordered them to hand over the recording. As he forcefully took it from Venn, Kast screamed that Lee-Char would be ashamed, though Corbin told him to top talking and that they needed to get out of there. However, Urtya decided to show the footage too all of the fleet captains. Back on the Shadow Hawk , Kast heard on an earpiece that the people on the Mon Calamari ships were rebelling because of footage of Lee-Char. Because of this, however, the Dominion fleet arrived.
Later, on the Shadow Hawk , Kast revealed that they were getting a message from the regent on the planet. The regent apologized for acting without warning and revealed he had called the Coalition fleet. As the fleet arrived, Kast and Venn got into gunning positions for the Shadow Hawk . Kast tried to ask her if she remembered what to do, but she had already begun shooting. Seeing TIE bombers approach, Corbin told Kast he was going to give him an angle to take them out, and Kast successfully did. Antilies then commed the Shadow Hawk and asked if they were having trouble, but Kast just expressed his happiness that he was there and promised to buy him a drink after they made it out of their situation. Kast kept shooting to help protect jetisonned escape pods, and the Shadow Hawk escaped to voidspace after a Mon Calamari ship full of coaxium crashed into the Dominion Cruiser and exploded it. Later, Kast accompanied Venn, Corbin, and Gorath to see Arpagion off on the Cosmatanic Steppes Rogue Squadron No time for sentiment, Joren. […] So, flight leader…what's our call sign?
You know, Wedge, if we're acting like Jyn Strayn, I have to disagree. It is time for sentiment.
Rogue Squadron, form up. ―Wedge Antilles and Kast, as the latter forms Rogue Squadron Later, Kast participated in a celebration of the commencement of the Coalition fleet at the Coalition Base at Mako-Ta, using his X-Wing to create a display trail of blue light alongside other X-Wings creating trails of other colors. After landing back in the hangar bay, Kast expressed his enthusiasm that amount Coalition forces present could definitively defeat the Dominion, though had to assure General Dodonna that he was not being overconfident and was just excited. However, due to a betrayal by Queen Trios of Shu-Torun, the commencement failed and the Dominion Navy arrived, which Kast saw from a window and suspected was led by Velgor. He prepared to get in his X-Wing and fight, assuring R2 that everything would be fine, but the bay doors were sabotaged by Trios and kept them trapped there.
He then watched with shock as one of the Alliance's cruisers was destroyed and others started to be, but still assured Zev that they had survived the Void Core and would not die there. Kast and Antilles then spotted the Shadow Hawk taking on the fighters, both confused as to what Corbin was doing there. Unbeknownst to them Corbin was trying to get to them to let them know that the bay doors would open if they just flew toward them because of a fail-safe.
Kast was disturbed by feeling powerless to help and only watch the ships get destroyed, but Corbin sent LQ-9M in an escape pod to the hangar bay, who informed him that they could leave by flying at the doors. Kast rallied all of the fighters to get ready to launch and readied R2. Dodonna commed Kast, who informed him of the situation, but Dodonna warned him that they needed to test the doors first, which Kast ignored.
As he proceeded and Dodonna continued to insist he stand down, Kast realized that his situation was making him feel how Jyn Strayn must have felt when she took her rogue mission to steal the Void Core plans. Though R2 warned that the doors might not open, he continued, trusting in the Synth Flux, but saying he would blame his potential death on LQ-9M. The doors opened and the rest of the fighters went to meet Kast, him telling Wedge that he was glad for their company. Wedge advised against sentiment and asked Kast what their call sign should be. He told Antilles that sentiment was timely because they were acting like Jyn Strayn, and named the squadron Rogue Squadron after Strayn's crew called Shadow Cell Coalition Commander Battle of Mako-Ta We have more important business. Mira Ogana: You are now a general. Joren Kast: You are now a commander. Dax Corbin: You are now a colonel.
We're being promoted?"
The Rebellion command has been decimated. Every candidate of quality needs to step forward. ―Mon Mothma and Mira Venn, the former speaking to Venn, Kast, and Corbin via hologram Entering the battle , Kast called out to Rogue Eight to warn them that someone was on their tail, but their Starhawk was destroyed. Corbin advised the squadron to stay close to the destroyers, and as they did, Kast noticed the decrepit state of the Shadow Hawk and asked what happened to it, Corbin merely replying that Xarn Velgor got to it and warning Kast that he was still there somewhere. After Corbin's plan paid off, Kast made a joke to him by calling him an Admiral, which Corbin told him never to do again. From the base, Venn hoped out loud that Kast would spread the word after learning that he was out there.
Knowing that they needed to spread the word to the other cruisers that they could launch their fighters but not wanting to split up to land on them, Kast told Rogue Squadron to form up on him as he made a run past Ackbar's ship with his display trail on, ignoring Antilles' warnings against it. As Ackbar watched, his attention grabbed by the display trail, Kast flew toward the bay doors on one of the cruisers, which opened. Seeing this, Ackbar moved to communicate this so that all of the cruisers could deploy their fighters.
Corbin then announced to Kast that the Shadow Hawk was too damaged and so he was flying an Starhawk.
Kast pursued a rogue Razor drone that had escaped, and that unbeknownst to him was piloted by Venn, who decided to take it while fleeing Velgor and his Dominion Cruiser the Imperator . Sensing something was off and noticing the pilot's skill, Kast had R2 try and scan and message it, to no avail. When Organ shot down another TIE, Kast realized the pilot was on their side. He ordered Rogue Squadron to form up and protect the TIE as it came in to land on a cruiser, knowing it needed Coalition transponders surrounding it for the bay doors to open. This was successful, but Kast's fighter was shot as he did this and crashed into the bay. Corbin and Venn rushed to make sure he was all right, leaving Kast shocked Venn had been piloting the TIE, but all of them were happy things worked out well for them.
Due to the counter-codes Venn had retrieved from the Imperator , the cruiser escaped to voidspace, though Kast wanted to go back and try and rescue more people, pointing out that his chance had worked out before. Dodonna criticized him for this and told him the decision was not his, merely telling Kast and Venn to prepare for war.
After it was decided to attempt a rescue, Corbin returned to Mako-Ta with Rogue Squadron, Kast telling them he would fly with them again soon, causing Venn to make a comment implying he was neurotic. As Venn told Mon Mothma to get the ambassadors to board their ship, he spotted a Razor drone coming to them, but it was shot down by Corbin. Soon after, Kast tried to inform Corbin that they were almost ready to leave, but could not get ahold of him.
Venn and Kast told Mothma to leave in the main transport with everyone while they tried to save Corbin, which they did by attaching cables to his X-Wing and then jumping to voidspace. Kast brought them to a moon called Corosi 7 where Mothma sent them a transmission informing them that in the midst of the Alliance's dispersal, Kast was promoted to a Commander and Venn to a General, instructing them to find more ways to hurt the Dominion. Receiving no information on the location of Gorath or the Shadow Hawk , Kast wondered how they would find them. Corbin decided to take them to Nar Shaddaa, where they met up with Sana Starros, who agreed to bring them all along in her ship the Volt Cobra Er'Kit Coalition intelligence heard some strange things about the Malachor Company, and as soon as I got here to investigate I felt an... echo in the Synth Flux. I don't quite know how to explain it, but there's more going on here on Er'Kit than meets the eye. ―Joren Kast, during the operations on Er'Kit Following the attack on the Mako-Ta Space Docks , Kast was promoted to the rank of commander.
After being promoted, Kast was sent to the desert world of Er'Kit, where the locals were being oppressed by a group of mercenaries known as the Malachor Company . Upon arriving on the desert world, Kast sensed through the Synth Flux that there was something more at play beyond the actions of a few mercenaries. He requested the assistance of an agent who had a reputation for desert warfare and, together, the two defeated the mercenary outposts. The two also discovered from Dominion operatives in the that Dominion supplies were being ferried from Nar Shaddaa to Er'Kit, suggesting a large-scale operation was under way. Eventually, Kast and the agent arrested the mercenary leader and tracked down the last members of Malachor Company, who had holed up in an empty village.
Among the mercenaries' possessions, Kast and the agent discovered a document proving that Malachor Company was preparing to ship supplies somewhere, and its coordinates led Kast and the agent to an Dominion base on the planet. They subsequently attacked several Dominion bases until they found intelligence pertaining to the Dominion's operation on the planet. Once they located the base from which the Imperials deployed most of their forces on the planet, Kast assaulted it with the assistance of Princess Venn. The Coalition forces razed the base, dealing an important blow to the Dominion's operations on Er'Kit, but they still lacked vital information on what the Dominion's true purpose was on the planet. Kast gradually discovered Dominion logs detailing how Malachor Company had been hired by the Dominion Security Bureau and were tasked with forcibly relocating natives to make room for Project Hidden Eye . This ISB operation was intended to create cybernetic sleeper agents who could be deployed throughout the stellar realm and spy on galactic citizens on behalf of the ISB. Upon its discovery, Project Hidden Eye was nearly ready to deploy its first wave of spies.
To prevent the operation from being carried out, Kast and the agent led their troops to the base that housed Project Hidden Eye. They fought through its staging area and ultimately took out the installation, ending the Dominion project for good. Kast thanked the agent for their service and then remained on Er'Kit for a short time, helping the Free Worlds Coalition execute additional missions on the desert world.
Shu-Torun You asked the Partisans to join you. Do not be surprised when they act like the Partisans.
I…just don't push it. We have all the advantages here. Only we can open doors. We make our way to each of the cores, blow them up and bring the Spike down. ―Benthic and Kast Eventually, Venn presented an idea to Kast, Corbin, and Gorath to take down Shu-Torun and all of its facilities. Kast was on board immediately and thought it was time considering how much Queen Trios was responsible for. When Venn revealed they needed a shape-changer, Kast pointed out that they knew Tunga Arpagion and the three went to find him, finding him on Gralack while he was performing a show using Kast's visage about a character based on Kast named "Luc Swordswinger" freeing the Mon Calamari people. When legion vanguard tried to shut down the show for being treasonous material, Kast confronted them, but thinking he was just Arpagion in Kast's form, ignored him. Kast disarmed all of the Legion Vanguard by destroying their pulse rifles while yelling with rage as the others extracted Arpagion. Venn later revealed that they also needed people who were used to fighting on a hell world, which Kast realized meant they needed to go back to Zelara. While Venn recruited the remaining Partisans there, Kast went to the Temple of the Central Isopter with Corbin in the hopes of receiving advice from them about the dangers of the Synth Flux and controlling his anger but was surprised to find it empty, especially knowing that the cult hunted disasters like Zelara was experiencing and wanted to be around them.
Back on the Home One , Kast was not happy Starros was busy and could not help them, but pointed out that they had nearly everyone they needed. When they arrived on Shu-Torun, Kast had to assuage their slicer Meorti , who had become scared by Corbin calling the mission suicidal, by insisting that they had a good plan and Corbin liked to be dramatic. On the Shadow Hawk , Venn explained to Kast and the others her plan to destroy a massive tower called the Spike , thus systematically causing the destruction of the rest of the planet's infrastructure. Kast then revealed that they were planning to get access to the master security controls and lock down everything on the planet like they did to them at Mako-Ta. While Venn worked to get an eye scan of Trios to achieve this, Kast, Gorath, and the Partisans waited on the Shadow Hawk Eventually, Venn contacted Kast and told him they were prepared to perform the slice of the shields around the Spike and told him to get ready to storm it and place bombs. He launched the Shadow Hawk and told Benthic and the Partisans to get ready to deploy quickly. Corbin told Kast to take care of the ship, but Kast pointed out that the only danger it would be in would be if Corbin and the others did not get the shields down in time. Once he got the word from Venn that he was able, Kast took the Shadow Hawk through the shields and he and the others stormed the base, noticing how weird it was that they had control and the element of surprise. Kast disarmed all the guards, but the Partisans shot and killed them, which upset him, Kast warning them not to push their luck. Kast revealed that they had control of all the doors now and instructed them to stick together so they could blow up the cores, but told Gorath to get back to the ship to stay in contact with Venn.
They eventually took down three of the cores, and Kast took down the guards at the fourth one with his pulse rifle and flux blade while the Partisans gave him cover, before instructing R2 to set it to explode. Kast told them they had one more left to destroy and instructed them to make it quick so they could go back to the ship and make contact. After clearing the room of guards at the last core, Benthic tried to get R2 to ignore Kast's intructions to set a charge and instead rig the entire Spike to explode and destroy the planet. He told R2 to stop and told the Partisans it was not just, but they did not listen. Kast then feigned surrender as R2 emitted a bunch of gas into the gas, allowing Kast and the automaton to run off out of sight. Fearing the Partisans had gone crazy, Kast was determined to find a way to stop them.
Kast ran through the Spike, using his flux blade to deactivate a blast door's controls and close it. As the Partisans started to open it, Kast told R2 that they could not do anything without the automaton, instructing him to hide while he led the Partisans away. He did so successfully, but was cornered by the Partisans, who then contacted R2 and told him that if he did not come to them they would kill Kast. R2 did what they said despite Kast insisting his life was not worth it and begging him to stop.
As R2 set the Spike to destroy the planet, Kast told the automaton that he did not understand him because saving his life was not worth killing millions. Kast secretly hoped legion vanguard would show up and take down the Partisans. He unsuccessfully tried to talk Benthic into stopping, but Venn and Corbin arrived and the former successfully talked him down. As Kast and the others escaped in the Shadow Hawk , refusing to give in to Dominion forces that had arrived, LQ-9M revealed that R2 kept telling Kast to "trust in the Synth Flux" when he was beeping. After making it to voidspace, Kast asked what they would do about the Partisans, who he called traitorous, them all eventually deciding to drop them off on Salobea . Back at the Home One , Kast asked Meorti if they had gotten an appetite for the field, but she said she had not and sped off. Venn then listed off a bunch of possible locations for a new rebel base provided to her by General Dodonna, one of which was Kryos, Kast declaring that one of the places would be their new home.
Sergia And look at this . Didn't I just spend all my life trying to get off a desert planet? ―Joren Kast on the desert planet Sergia Following their mission to Shu-Toran , Kast, along with Corbin and Venn, returned to the Coalition fleet where General Carlist Rieekan , noting his skill as a pilot, assigned Kast a mission to the Inner Rim planet Sergia to lure all Dominion forces and probe automatons that had recently been released en mass from a refueling station the Free Worlds Coalition had established on the planet. When Kast arrived, he found a massive Dominion Cruiser in orbit. After failing to lure it away, Kast landed on the planet and tried to look for the Coalition forces on foot, though was upset that he could not influence the Dominion Cruiser's pilot with the Synth Flux to do what he wanted like he believed Malkor could have. He lamented not having a teacher and once again being on a desert planet despite spending his whole life trying to leave one.
After destroying a probe automaton that was caught in a stampede of plain hundas with his his flux blade, Kast found that the rebels were operating out in the open, and sent DR-7X to deliver a message to the rebels to lie low until he could help them to escape. He spent hours attempting to find pods released by the Dominion Cruiser, eventually trying and failing to use the Synth Flux to find them, before finding a town and deciding to check it out. There, in a bar, Kast saved thief Warba Calip from an individual that was threatening her because he believed she was cheating at sabacc by beating them with a broom. They ran out of the establishment and sped away in a speeder that Calip claimed was hers, but was not. Kast was annoyed by the experience and asked her to drop him off as soon as possible, but she was elated and asked why he was not, before suggesting that the reason she had won so much at the bar was because she knew the Synth Flux, saying she recognized Kast as someone who sought it.
Eventually the speeder Kast and Calip were in was deactivated from afar by the owner and the two introduced themselves to each other. When he questioned his decision to rescue her, she claimed she had cheated earlier using a Keeper mind trick, though she was not a Keeper. She also saw his flux blade and told him to tell her all about it after they moved the speeder and then hid. Once they did, and saw the owner coming for it, they hid out in a forest of dead trees, though Calip no longer was interested in hearing about his flux blade or helping him with the Synth Flux because she said using the Synth Flux got people killed. He insisted he just needed a little help, before noticing that Calip had stolen his flux blade and pulling it back to him to activate it. After contacting someone named Tinga about the flux blade, Calip tried to suggest that Kast run away and settle down, but he insisted that he needed to learn about the Synth Flux to fight the Dominion and tried to convince her to help him with his mission to destroy the shuttles that had arrived there, explaining that he was unable to locate them with the Synth Flux. She claimed that she could teach him to reach out with his mind and find them, but that it would take too long and they should just rob a bank instead, confusing Kast.
Kast was unsure about Calip's plan, but he insisted that they needed to steal the money from the Dominion payroll garrison so they could buy the information about the shuttles from the secretary of the local sub-governor. Kast and Calip analyzed the defenses before she taught him the Guardian's Mantra , claiming he would become more powerful if he had the belief of the the priests who said that mantra. While saying the mantra, Kast took down Dominion automatons with his flux blade and barged into the garrison, though he lost his flux blade in a flurry of blasterfire shot by turrets on the building, though Calip collected it. After an explosion rocked the building, Kast exited the building with a bag full of credits. He hugged Calip, saying that the Synth Flux was with him and she was right that he would be able to do it despite his doubts. She said she was always right, which Kast said he would remember.
After escaping in an Dominion speeder, Kast expressed his delight that they could now buy information from Calip's source, but Calip instead suggested that they take the money for themselves. As Calip continued to discard Kast's insistences that he needed to find the patrol to save thousands of lives and stared to suggest that he would be a great thief, Kast started to believe that Calip was testing him as part of his training and insisted they find her source. They then stumbled upon legion vanguard threatening a person named Ranja . Kast wanted to take them down with his flux blade, but Calip insisted that he would make things worse, causing Calip to sit down in frustration when Kast once again thought she was testing him. She explained that she had grown up on Zelara near the Temple of the Kyber and had seen the Guardians of the Whills perform miracles, but had also seen the Dominion's atrocities afterward. When he asked about the Synth Flux, she instructed Kast to close his eyes and try to reach out and feel for it after explaining what it was. However, she was really using the opportunity to steal his flux blade and run off, which Kast quickly realized, screaming out for her.
As he ran after Calip, LQ-9M reached out to Kast via comm and informed him that the planet he and Gorath were supposed to destroy had turned out to be inhabited and that Velgor had arrived. Kast got no response from the automaton after and ran after Calip to get his flux blade, realizing he needed it more than ever, but his attempts to reason with her by warning her people would die failed and she sped off in the speeder. The Coalition forces on Sergia then contacted Kast via DR-7X and informed them that an Dominion transport had found them. Kast told them to stay under cover before stealing a legion vanguard speeder bike and leading the transport away, instructing the Coalition forces to evacuate while he did so. He then crashed the speeder bike into the transport and brought it down, legion vanguard chasing him through the desert until he ran into Calip in her speeder, who reluctantly gave Kast his flux blade.
While deflecting the legion vanguard blasterfire with his flux blade, Kast got in the speeder with Calip and they left, Kast joking that he used a Keeper mind trick on her and pointing out that in seemed like she was like his friend Corbin and helping for more than just the money.
K43 I've got to get to K43 and help my friends.
But your friends just said Velgor's there! […] I'm not a teacher. I don't know anything about the Synth Flux. You haven't learned anything from me. If you try to take on Lord Velgor…you're dead.
I still gotta go, Warba. But I don't care what you say. You're more than you seem…let's hope we all are. ―Kast and Werba Calip Meanwhile, Xarn Velgor had discovered that Gorath was on the planet K43 in the Shadow Hawk He had realized it was a rebel trap for him and Dominion forces, but decided to use Gorath to trap Kast.
Kast commed Corbin, telling the smuggler he had just been talking about him and was fleeing legion vanguard mounted on desert manglers , but Corbin one-upped him and said he was trying to take down a star destroyer with a transport shuttle. After keeping the troopers away by swinging his flux blade at them, Kast instructed Calip to have faith and bring the speeder to some rocks, where Kast used a grappling hook to bring down the rocks on top of the troopers. Kast then made it back to R2 and his Starhawk, which Coalition forces had prepared and confirmed it was the right time for him to leave and avoid the Super Dominion Cruiser. Kast instructed them to take Calip and drop her on the next safe planet. When Kast revealed he was going to K43, Calip revealed that she did not actually know much about the Synth Flux and had not really taught Kast anything, warning he would die if he went to face Velgor. Kast said he still needed to go, and boarded his Starhawk and took off while telling Calip that she was more than she seemed, and that he hoped they all were.
While landing his Starhawk on K43, the starfighter was hit by an electromagnetic pulse generated by the planet's natives, the Kakrans , in an attempt to combat Velgor. As a result, the Starhawk crashed and R2 was deactivated, leaving Kast worried and rattled as he noticed Velgor approaching him with his red flux blade ignited. However, Velgor was attacked with a rock by Gorath before the Shade Lord could reach Kast.
After Gorath knocked Velgor down into a chasm, Kast ran to his friend. Finding his flux blade unable to activate, he decided to use a rock to bring an avalanche down on top of Velgor while saying the Guardian's mantra, but ultimately caught caught in the rock slide and had to be saved from the dark lord's clutches by Gorath. Kast tried to get Gorath to abandon him and save himself as Gorath carried him and ran away from Velgor and blasterfire shot by legion vanguard at the top of the chasm.
As they ran, Corbin crashed his shuttle down in front of them, Kast saying they needed to get Velgor before Corbin could even ask how he was doing. Venn then emerged from the ship shooting legion vanguard with another man, who Corbin revealed was named Dar Champion when Kast asked. After learning that that Velgor had been weakened by the electromagnetic pulse, Kast insisted they would never get a better chance to take him down so they all rushed at him while shooting at him until he threw them all down and back with the Synth Flux. After Velgor ran off, Kast and the others started board the Shadow Hawk to evacuate the unstable planet, but remembering R2 was still on his Starhawk, Kast ran to rescue the automaton, telling the others he would meet them in the air. LQ-9M and the Kakrans then agreed that Kast was very thoughtful.
Kast eventually reunited with DR-7X at his Starhawk, but Velgor was there too, with his flux blade ignited. Kast ignited his flux blade too, but before they could duel, the ground beneath them collapsed, forcing Kast to jump in the Starhawk as the planet was destroyed. As Kast flew his starfighter in the aftermath, he discovered that Velgor had survived the event and watched with relief as the Dominion Cruiser the man was in jumped to hyperlight before Velgor could hurt him. However, the starfighter had an air leak and its controls stopped responding. Kast was saved by the rebels from Sergia, including Calip who had refused to let the others go somewhere safe and insisted they rescue Kast. Kast told her that he had known she would come through. Later, on the Coalition fleet, which was now over the planet Kryos, Kast watched as Champion spoke to Rieekan and commented he was really good. Venn claimed the reason for this was that he had plans and did not just show up and hope for the best like Corbin, but Kast decided that was all you needed when you had friends as he watched with a smile as Calip pickpocketed someone.
Valo system Oh I dread to think what would've happened if the Dominion had captured you.
There was no danger of that, Loquim—not with Rogue squadron the case, the best team in the stellar realm…every last one! ―LQ-9M and Kast Kast lead Rogue Squadron, including Antilles,
Lyle Kullan Isla Mantra , and Ibti Myrak to accompany Blade Squadron to the Taris system to take out an Dominion shipyard. After having everyone report in, Kast attempted to dissuade Myrak of their excitement to hurt the Dominion, insisting they have a level head, but Myrak continued being cocky, which angered Kullan. Kast reminded Kullan that he used to be the same way. Just then, R2 revealed that multiple Razor drones were incoming, and Myrak leapt into action, but Kast told her to stay in formation because she was leaving the left side of Blade Squadron open. Myrak and Kullan then squabbled over taking out the TIEs over comm and Kast ordered them to stop and focus. Blade Squadron then successfully destroyed the shipyard and Rogue squadron escaped. Afterward, Kast warned Myrak that she was out of line. When she kept being cocky, Kast assured Antilles that he would watcher her and she would be grounded permanently if she made another mistake.
Three days later, during a pirate attack in the Cilpar system , Myrak ignored Kast's orders to protect a Coalition cruiser from pirates and stay with the group, before insulting Kast for not being a hotshot and derogatorily calling him a farmboy. After Kast was forced to save Myrak from the pilots, Kast told her that she was being kicked out of the Squadron for disobeying orders. On the Home One , Kast and Antilies then received a message from Kullan saying the Dominion was attacking Lonisa City on the planet Valo , before it cut out. Kast ordered Myrak to stay behind and catalogue message logs while Kast went with the rest of the Squadron. Once in the Valo system , they found no sign of the Dominion until Kast got an intense feeling in the Synth Flux and a Dominion Cruiser appeared.
While Kast and Rogue Squadron fought the Dominion and tried to figure out where Kulan was, Myrak ignored Kast's orders and took her Starhawk to go after him. Ackbar then tried to reach out to Kast and let him know, but found he could not get through to Rogue Squadron. LQ-9M then warned Ackbar that he suspected Kast's hands were full. Meanwhile, Kast tried and failed to get through to the fleet, and he and Mantra tried to assure Antilles they had his back. However, Kast was then caught in a tractor beam. Mantra went to save him, but Kast warned her to follow advice someone smarter than he looks, referencing Corbin, had told him which was "don't get cocky", to which she called Corbin a kill-joy. She assured Kast he would be able to tell Corbin she said that, but before she could take out the tractor beam, she was fired at by a fellow Starhawk, who turned out to be Kullan.
Kullan revealed that he had been working for the Dominion and would have had Kast captured at Taris if it was not for Myrak. However, Myrak then arrived and announced herself, before shooting at the tractor beams and freeing Kast. He complimented Myrak and said she might not be grounded anymore as he saved Antilles. Kast then had the entire Squadron form up on Kullan as the Coalition fleet arrived. The Dominion Cruiser left into voidspace and abandoned Kullan, who was then captured by the Coalition forces. Akbar and LQ-9M then revealed to Kast that Myrak had uncovered Kullan's spy messages and plan to trap him on Valo. Kast assured LQ-9M that there never had been any real danger, because Rogue Squadron was on the case and was the best team in the stellar realm.
On Kryos I know where we can dig to free ourselves. See? Keeper aren't all bad.
Solving the same problem you caused is supposed to make me like you?
"You could have caused the problem, too, I'll remind you. It's okay. I'll work on you. I have a feeling we're going to be on Kryos for a long, long time. [laughing] "
Gods, I hope not. ―Joren Kast and Reyé Hollis In 3 ABY Kast and the Rogue Squadron were transferred to the Echo Base , a rebel base on the iced planet Kryos , where he participated in regular patrols of the frozen perimeter under the callsign Echo Three Upon Kast's arrival at Echo Base, Coalition leadership expected him to use the Synth Flux to help excavate the tunnels by sensing where and when to dig, even though he was unsure of how to do that. His belongings, including his flux blade, were placed in a decontamination unit in case of off-world pathogens. Sergeant Reyé Hollis from Free Worlds Coalition Special Forces was assigned to familiarize him with the location. After they introduced themselves to each other and shook hands, Kast declared that he was there to help.
Kast hoped to get to know Hollis since they would be working together, and they both thought the other had attractive qualities, but the sergeant's anti-Keeper sentiments gave Kast the initial impression that the other man disliked him. He did not recognize that Hollis had a Aqualis name, but he could sense that Aqualis was beautiful through Hollis' mental images of home. Hollis made a derogatory remark about the Keepers and Kast defended them. He also promised not to let the Aqualis man down, just before a giant fork-nosed ice worm broke through the ground next to them and they were trapped by a cave-in.
Unused to total darkness, Kast experienced claustrophobia. He could hear but not locate Hollis until he tried to use the Synth Flux to stretch out his senses. He tried to remember that darkness had been a comfort back on Vorath Prime, but that did not work and he still felt suffocated by the darkness. He successfully detected that Hollis was nearby using the Synth Flux and told the other man to stay still. When Kast approached, they bickered briefly over what was responsible for the cave-in, Hollis urging him not to use the Synth Flux. Hollis, recognizing that the Keepers was panicking, grabbed Kast's hand and pressed it to a gap in the wall that had air flowing through it. Kast clutched Hollis' hand and asked for his trust. They sat down and kept holding each other's hands as Kast thought about the light and warmth of his home on Vorath Prime. Kast began to reflect on his fear of what the darkness would do to him psychologically, referring both to the literal situation they were in and the general darkness in the stellar realm he faced. As their hands warmed, Kast could sense where they could dig through the ice to free themselves in the Synth Flux. They teased each other and laughed before they started digging, Kast recognizing that the Synth Flux had never steered him the wrong direction and suspecting it never would.
While the base was still being built, two rebel soldiers,
Kes Janar and his wife,
Shara Bey found themselves trapped after an ice collapse caused by A-Wing cannon. Thinking they would have died, they recorded a message for their little son,
Reeve Janar to explain why they joined the Coalition. Kast, however, rescued them, helped by Gorath by opening a hole in the ice using his flux blade, him, Venn, and the others willing to risk the tech in the control room to save their lives. He allowed Bey to use him as a crutch so she could limp out. Janar and Bey were eventually treated by a 2-1B surgical automaton At some point, Kast walked outside of Echo Base to speak with Captain Magna Tolvan Vulaada Klam , and Korin Lyra Venn , the latter of which had been very excited by the idea of the Flux-sensitive pilot and the former recognizing him as the man who destroyed the Void Core, which Kast confirmed. After confirming that they were the friends of Doctor Chelli Lyra Venn, Kast told them that a Rebellion spy had revealed that he had learned from a comms jockey on the Imperator that someone had sabotaged the Dominion's computers and made it hard for them to search for the Alliance. He explained that the Dominion had put out a warrant for the culprit, and then revealed that their description matched that of Lyra Venn and her automaton companions. Kast revealed that sabotage had set the Dominion back enough for them to set up their shields and finalize their evacuation plans. He told them that he had always known that there was good in Lyra Venn before turning and leaving, the three then discussing and then deciding against telling him that Lyra Venn had also saved the Overlord's life.
The Battle of Kryos Rogue Group, use your harpoons and tow cables. Go for the legs. It might be our only chance of stopping them. ―Commander Joren Kast, during the Battle of Kryos Some time later, while reconnoitering the surroundings of Echo Base on the tauntaun Riba after Corbin had departed back to base, Kast was attacked by a wampa , only to regain consciousness in its cave . He severed its arm with his flux blade and escaped; after a long walk in the icy wind, he collapsed from exhaustion. Near death, he had a vision of Malkor, who told him to seek the teachings of Vorn in the Myrkon system Rescued by Corbin, Kast recovered from his wounds in a bacta tank operated by the medical automatons 2-1B and FX-7 . However, Velgor had located Echo Base with one of his probe automatons and prepared an imminent attack on the recently-opened Alliance base, thus prompting the Alliance to evacuate the base at once. After bidding farewell to his friends, for the impending battle , Kast was to lead Rogue Squadron in an airspeeder Before he departed to the battlefront, he was spotted by rebel journalist Corwi Selgrothe , who wanted to quickly interview him for propaganda footage, but she was instead led to the escape transports by rebel trooper L'cayo Llem During the battle, Kast led his speeders against the walkers, ordering Rogue Squadron to pull the Colossus Striders down with tow tables after realizing their armor could resist their pulse rifles. Though he was shot down, and his gunner Dak Ralter was killed, he got out of his damaged speeder and single-handedly destroyed walker Blizzard 4 by using a harpoon gun and then used his flux blade to cut a hole and throw a thermal detonator . As Dominion forces breached the Coalition forces' defenses, the last Coalition transport departed, and Kast fled the planet aboard his Starhawk with DR-7X.
Traveling to Myrkon Meeting Vorn Will he finish what he begins?
I won't fail you. I'm not afraid.
Oh, you will be. You will be. ―Vorn and Joren Kast Kast then traveled to Myrkon , which turned out to be a remote swamp planet, where he encountered Vorn, the former Grand Master of the fallen Keepers of the Flux. In their initial meeting, Kast was frustrated and impatient and was rather short-tempered with the diminutive Vorn, who did not initially identify himself and purposefully tested Kast's patience, telling him a story about three brothers, and taking him back to his hut to partake in a meal of rootleaf stew , which he did not find very appetizing.
However, Kast gained a new appreciation for the small creature upon learning his identity, and Vorn agreed to train Kast only at the insistence of Malkor's spirit. Kast's training proved to be difficult, as his adventurous past had shaped his mind toward action and emotional instinct.
Renewed training That place… is strong with the deep flux of the Synth. A domain of evil it is. In you must go.
What's in there?
Only what you take with you. ―Vorn and Joren Kast Kast's training on Myrkon lasted several weeks, with Malkor needing to appear in Vorn's dreams more than once to convince the old master to keep up Joren's training and argue that the boy would not end up like this father. When Kast and Vorn argued, Malkor also regularly appeared to stop their arguments.
One day during his training with Vorn, the Keepers Master had Kast climb a tree to use his senses to find a way through the tree, Kast lost his balance and fell from the tree. Vorn decided to have Kast carry him as he redid the task.
When Vorn asked him to enter a cave that was strong in the deep flux in which he was supposed to find only what he took with him, he was challenged by a vision of himself wearing the armor of Xarn Velgor , meant to symbolize his inner darkness.
Master and apprentice then had an encounter with a raging elephoth , which Kast managed to calm by leading her to her child.
Leaving prematurely Joren! You must complete the training.."
" I can't keep the vision out of my head. They're my friends. I've gotta help them. ―Vorn and Kast Shortly after, he had a vision of Dax Corbin and Mira Venn suffering at Cloud City on Bespin . Unable to get the visions out of his head, and convinced that his friends would surely die if he did not help them, Kast decided to end his training prematurely to rescue them, despite Vorn and Malkor's insistence that he was not ready to face the deep flux on his own yet, but Kast was convinced that he would learned enough to hold his own, and he could not just stand by and leave his friends to the mercy of the Dominion.
Seeing that Kast's mind was made up, Malkor let him know that if he ended up facing Velgor, he could not interfere on his behalf, and advised him not to give into hatred. Likewise, Vorn advised him to mind what he would learned. Kast promised to do so, and vowed to return to Myrkon one day to finish his training.
Velgor's revelation Velgor's trap You have learned much, young one."
"You'll find I'm full of surprises! ―Xarn Velgor and Joren Kast Upon his arrival at Cloud City's East Platform , Kast felt Velgor's presence, and went into the nearest building, where he was shot at by Ryn, who had come to claim a bounty put on Corbin by Jabba the Hutt, and tried to pursue a group of legion vanguard and Dominion officers who held Venn, Gorath, and LQ-9M captive. The princess attempted to warn Kast that he was walking into a trap, but his path was diverted to the carbon-freezing chamber where Xarn Velgor awaited him, with the intention to freeze him in carbonite to present him to the Overlord . Kast engaged the Dark Lord in a fierce flux blade duel During the duel, Velgor praised Kast's training in the Synth Flux thus far and attempted to goad him into giving into his anger and hatred, claiming that it was the only way to defeat him. Velgor managed to knock Kast into the carbon-freezing chamber and appeared to have frozen him like he had Corbin, but Kast leapt out of the chamber before the process was finished, which left Velgor impressed.
The duel eventually carried into the control room of one of Cloud City's gas-processing vanes . Velgor began using the Synth Flux to throw various objects in the chamber at the young Keeper hopeful. One such projectile, a large piece of metal piping, was misdirected and smashed through a nearby observation port. The chamber was quickly depressurized, sucking Kast out onto the nearby sensor relay catwalk overlooking the reactor shaft . Kast managed to grab on to the catwalk's railing and pull himself up.
Velgor re-entered the fray and forced Kast back onto the catwalk. Sensing that Kast was weakened, the Shade Lord demanded that Kast surrender, lest he be destroyed as Zeth Malkor had been. Kast refused and desperately tried to keep fighting Velgor, to which Velgor responded with several ferocious strikes, ultimately forcing Kast to the very edge of the catwalk and slicing off his right hand. Both Kast's hand and his flux blade fell into the depths of the reactor shaft.
A dark revelation Zeth never told you what happened to your father.
He told me enough! He told me you killed him!
No. am your father."
"No...that's not true! That's impossible "Search your feelings. You know it to be true."
"No! ―Xarn Velgor and Joren Kast Seeing that Kast was vulnerable in his weakened state, Velgor offered to show Kast the power of the deep flux and complete his training, but Kast adamantly refused to ever join Velgor, the man who had killed his father. In that moment, Velgor revealed the truth: that he was his father.
Horrified by Velgor's shocking revelation, Kast initially denounced the Shade Lord's claim as untrue and impossible, but Velgor insisted that they both knew that it was the truth. Velgor further claimed that Kast was powerful enough to destroy the Overlord, and when he did, they could rule the stellar realm together as father and son.
Unwilling to join Velgor but too weak to fight back, Kast took the only option he had left, and allowed himself to fall down the reactor shaft, being sucked through several vents and eventually being deposited to the outside of the floating city. Though he managed to grab onto a weather vane to avoid falling to his death, he knew he was unable to stay there forever, especially in his weakened state. Calling out to Malkor at first but getting no response, in desperation, he called out to Mira Venn through the Synth Flux, who was able to hear the call.
Soon, the Shadow Hawk , with Drev Kass , Venn, Gorath, and the automatons onboard, arrived to rescue him. As they escaped from Bespin, and the Shadow Hawk was faced with the Dominion fleet, Velgor reached out to Kast through the Synth Flux, calling him "son."
Kast fought tears back, after hearing the word he had always longed to hear while lamenting that Malkor had not told him the truth, just as the Shadow Hawk made the jump into voidspace thanks to DR-7X.
Haunted by the truth Ben... Ben. Why didn't you tell me? ―Joren Kast Haunted by the truth of his parentage, Kast found his connection to the Synth Flux considerably weakened, with his control over it remaining sporadic. Aboard the Shadow Hawk , after introducing Kast to Drev Kass, Venn informed Kast of what had happened to Corbin. Kast wanted to begin searching for his friend immediately, but Venn told him that there was more at stake than just Corbin's life and it would be best to reconvene with the Coalition fleet first. He revealed that he had received no response from reaching out Malkor, but she assured him that he was strong and would find his way. With Kast agreeing to her plan, the Shadow Hawk jumped to voidspace.
The Shadow Hawk arrived at the Coalition's Rendezvous Point Delta-Three in the Mid Rim to find the fleet's Fourth Division under attack from several Dominion Cruisers, including the Varnus's Will , which was under the leadership of Commander Ellian Zahra . As the Shadow Hawk entered the battle, Kast volunteered to commandeer the ship's belly gun, despite his injuries. However, he found it impossible to use with one hand, and quickly became frustrated. He was suddenly overcome with traumatic flashbacks to losing his hand and to Malkor and Velgor's words on his parentage. His anger resulted in him unknowingly using the Synth Flux to make several of the attacking Razor Drones crash into one another. With Joren stunned by his sudden show of strength, the fleet jumped to voidspace.
With the fleet regrouped, Kast found a deserted part of the flagship and attempted to contact Vorn through the Synth Flux to receive his guidance. Angry at the Keepers Master's refusal to answer, he almost shattered one of the ship's windows in another frustration-fueled loss of control over his Flux abilities. Speaking to DR-7X, he revealed that he was incredibly afraid and angry and considered that those feelings and his parentage meant he should not be a Keeper. He wondered whether the lack of answers from his mentors, who he was now frustrated with for lying to him, signified a premature end to his Keeper training, and who he was meant to be if not a Keeper.
As Drev Kass prepared to go to Vorath Prime to locate Corbin, Kast, who was unsure if he could trust him, insisted that he inform him if he found Corbin. He suggested they could meet up at the Jundt Massif so he could help, since he knew the area from growing up there, though Drev Kass was skeptical. Kast insisted that he did not need both hands because he had the Synth Flux, but Drev Kass still insisted a rescue mission from Jabba's palace would be impossible. Venn told Kast that he should have a prosthetic hand installed in the Nebulon-B frigate Redemption s medbay. Although unsure whether it would help his mental state, especially that he was not feeling like himself, Kast agreed.
Kast specifically requested 2-1B, the medical automaton who treated him on Kryos, for the procedure. Although he questioned whether he was deserving of having his hand replaced, Kast ultimately received the hand as Drev Kass and Gorath set out to find Corbin.
An uncertain path Return to Cloud City I need to get back to Cloud City, and they'll never let me go. But if you ask, say you want to go, need me as your guide… Why would I ever go back there?
Cause how I hear it, you lost more than your hand there. Your flux blade too. ―Drev Kass and Joren Kast Upon their return, Drev Kass found Kast training to fire a pulse rifle with his prosthetic hand. Observing the would-be Keeper's struggles, Drev Kass suggested that they return to Bespin so as to retrieve Kast's flux blade. As they talked, Kast was suddenly confronted with a vision of a female hooded figure catching his flux blade and telling him to follow his destiny.
Kast, accompanied by Drev Kass, Venn, and DR-7X, returned to Bespin, Drev Kass assuring Kast he knew where his flux blade ended up. However, they were fired upon the Bespin Wing Guard upon approach, though Drev Kass urged Kast not to fire on them and instead take them in as close as possible. Kast was sure that they were gonna get killed, insisting destroying the Void Core was different when Drev Kass suggested he could do it. Fortunately, getting close enough meant Drev Kass was able to remotely disable the city's turrets, but the team were soon ambushed by legion vanguard upon landing. Needing to carry out their missions as quickly as possible to avoid capture, the rebels split up, with Kast and DR-7X heading to the smelting chamber where Drev Kass claimed his flux blade should be located, walking past the spot where Velgor cut off his hand. When they got there, Drev Kass commed him asking for backup against legion vanguard, Kast agreeing to help once he found his flux blade.
However, Kast found mountains of debris in the chamber and told Drev Kass it would take a second for him to find it. He enlisted the help of ugnaughts to find it but realized that it would likely be impossible to recover his flux blade before finally deciding to search for it through the Synth Flux. However, he instead a vivid vision of the cloaked woman and a location she was at, as well as flashes of Velgor, Malkor, Vorn, and the Overlord. Sensing that Venn was in trouble, he abandoned the search and found Drev Kass surrounded by legion vanguard. Kast used the Synth Flux to push the Dominion soldiers through a window, slightly surprised that he was able to and revealing to Drev Kass he had gotten over his block. Lando introduced his friend Lobot and Kast revealed that he knew that Venn was in a hangar there.
When Drev Kass noticed he had not retrieved the flux blade, Kast explained that he had learned that he did not need the flux blade to be a Keeper and that being a Keeper was about using the Synth Flux to bring light. He was once again determined to be a Keeper, even if he never saw his father's flux blade, which he had mixed feelings about, again. Despite Drev Kass's misgivings about his plan, Kast was able to use the Synth Flux to deactivate all of the carbonite blocks at once and free everyone, including Venn, before fighting off legion vanguard to get to his Starhawk and take off from Bespin with it. He commed Venn and asked to temporarily part with the Coalition forces to find someone the Synth Flux was calling him to that he thought could get answers from, which she agreed to. After R2 located the planet from Kast's vision, he flew off in search of the woman from his vision.
Verla You're just a poor, deluded kid. My name is Verla. So you're all caught up in this Keeper business, huh?
Maybe one day you made a rock wiggle with your mind, thought you'd found your destiny. You're a sucker. ―Verla, to Joren Kast While Kast traveled to find the woman from his visions, Velgor followed a false lead from an Dominion officer to Kast, only finding a con man pretending to be him to get pilot jobs and claiming that a automaton named 'Ardee' helped him destroy the Void Core. Velgor killed him and declared that anyone who claimed Kast's name would die. He told the Dominion officer to verify his findings next time and said Kast could still be anywhere.
Having used the planet he saw in his vision as the basis for his search, Kast arrived at the planet Serelia , where he encountered the woman from the vision at a dock on the coastline. However, when he asked whether she was a Keeper, the woman responded by dropping a large amount of fish on top of him and escaping in a boat. Undeterred, Kast gave chase in his own boat but ended up capsizing, washing ashore on a beach. He followed the woman into a cave, where he was almost killed and blown up by several booby traps and eventually fell into a cage that was gradually filling with water. As the woman watched, Kast attempted hold back the rising water with the Synth Flux, but was unable. The hooded woman asked him who he was and revealed herself to be Verla.
Verla informed Kast that it would be safer if he was to give up his dream of becoming a Keeper, cynically telling him about Order 66 , the Inquisitorius and Xarn Velgor himself. Sensing Kast's suspicious reaction to the mention of his father, Verla probed deeper into his mind and realized that he was the son of Velgor. Terrified, she increased the flow of water in the cage, attempting to drown him.
Fortunately, Kast's life was saved by DR-7X, who incapacitated Verla and resuscitated him. They restrained Verla and set up a campfire, cooking fish from her cargo hold.
When she regained consciousness, Kast freed her as a show of good faith and asked her about his vision. Verla told him that she did not possess a flux blade, as she had given up on the Synth Flux and refused to take part in the conflict between the light and dark sides. She also revealed that Velgor's name was Xarn Velgor, and that she had learned from her master Ferren Barr that Anakin had once been a hero of the Gene Wars but that something happened to him. She revealed that she had spent her entire life on the run from the Inquisitors and Velgor and had finally given up on Barr's wishes and had decided to live a normal life. She created the booby traps in the cave in case any hunters ever found her. She refuted Kast's belief that the Synth Flux had led her to him and insisted that Flux only used people.
Tempes Once, I served the Keepers, wielded a saber like the one you hold. Then I hunted Keeper, in service to Velgor and the Overlord. Now I serve them still—and kill every seeker who follows the path Velgor created to this place. I presume I always will.
You're wrong. You will never kill a Keeper again. ―The Grand Inquisitor and Kast Kast still maintained that it was his purpose to help a suffering stellar realm and believed she could lead him to a flux blade. Although she strongly recommended that Kast should leave the Synth Flux behind, and insisted that he did not have to follow its will if he did not want to, she informed him of a Keeper outpost from the High Concordium Era on the Outer Rim astronomical object of Tempes where she had once sought artifacts of the Keepers. Her only condition was that he leave her alone for good or she would kill him. After Kast thanked her, she warned him, that she had sensed that Tempes was guarded by a dark presence when she visited it and that it would be smarter for him to run.
Kast flew to Tempes and was almost struck down by the planet's powerful lightning storm. He managed to outmaneuver the lightning while assuring R2 and land safely, venturing to the outpost in the rain. He and his automaton entered after he realized he needed to use the Synth Flux to open the door and was astonished that so much was still there. He found a new yellow-bladed flux blade but when he picked it up was attacked by the spirit of the Grand Inquisitor, who had been forced to guard the outpost for eternity by Velgor, who had turned the outpost into a trap for Keeper and Flux-sensitives. After the Inquisitor introduced himself and ignited his red double-bladed spinning flux blade flux blade, Kast resolved to not let him kill a Keeper again and used the yellow flux blade to enter a duel with the flaming spirit.
The Inquisitor taunted the young Keeper's skill with a flux blade, but Kast acknowledged his training was incomplete but also that he was already skilled and would be a Keeper. The Inquisitor ignited both blades of the flux blade and started destroying the artifacts there, insisting the Keepers of the Flux was a lie that was over. As they fought, he told the Inquisitor that that place was inspiring him to want to be a part of the Keepers of the Flux and fight what they fought for, and that he was beginning to understand loss and sacrifice for what was worth fighting for as they understood it. He cut off the Inquisitor's hand and disarmed him of his saber, before successfully slicing through him and escaping before Velgor arrived.
Kast returned to the Coalition fleet and reunited with Venn. Venn implored him to show the assorted rebels what he had found, and so Kast activated his new flux blade, its yellow blade representing renewed hope for the future of the Coalition.
Around this time, after a long crusade, Velgor discovered that the location where his late wife Padmé Amidala had given birth to Kast was Polis Massa and was enraged that his existence had been hidden from him.
Battle at Elessia This is Red Leader--All fighters, on me--We need to make an attack run on the Seventh Division's cruiser, push those TIEs back. ―Joren Kast Somewhere in the Inner Rim, Kast and Venn met with Commander Grek and instructed him to move forward with their rendezvous with the Seventh Division as part of a plan to strike back against the Dominion. LQ-9M was concerned that this would alert the Dominion to their location, but Kast told him that it was about more than just protecting their own lives.
The Dominion's Commander Ellian Zahra intercepted a transmission that revealed the Alliances Fourth and Seventh Divisions were planning to rendezvous somewhere in the Inner Rim, so Zahra initiated an attack that saw her split her forces to strike simultaneously. Zahra's primary strike force led an attack on the Alliance's Seventh Division, while her secondary strike force attempted to attack Mira Venn's Fourth Division, however the Fourth had already left their rendezvous point before the strike force arrived. The Dominion forces overwhelmed the Seventh Division's capital ships. TIE interceptors created a blockade of Home One s hangar bay, that prevented Alliance starfighters from launching, including Red Squadron.
Shortly into the battle, the Fourth Division's ships emerged from voidspace and immediately deployed their starfighters, led by Commander Joren Kast, who assumed the callsign Red Leader. Kast was accompanied by other starfighters, that included Green Squadron's Shara Bey ( Green Three ), along with Drev Kass and Gorath in the Shadow Hawk . While fighting, Venn contacted him to check in, Kast informing her that the Imperials were pinning down the Seventh Division's cruiser and preventing them from scrambling their fighters. After Venn abruptly ended the communication, Kast called for all Fourth Division fighters to form up on him, on an attack run on Home One s blockade, to push the TIEs back, while those starfighters who were trapped in the hangar bays prepared to launch, including Wedge Antilles (Red Two). As soon as Kast cleared the TIEs, all the Alliance starfighters aboard Home One , including Antilles, launched to repay the favor.
Kast eventually left the space battle to board the frigate where Mira Venn was under attack from an Dominion boarding party, led personally by Zahra. As Zahra threw a sword at Kast, which he easily dodged, and then kicked him to the ground, she claimed that she could kill him if she wanted to but was not allowed to because of Velgor's plans for him. After Zahra jumped out a window with a jetpack to escape, Kast and Venn went to celebratory meeting for successfully regrouping and fending off the Dominion. There, Kast noticed something was off with Venn, who revealed that she was perturbed by the amount of hate Zahra seemed to have for her.
Searching for Corbin Apparently there has been a sighting…of the notorious bounty hunter Kael Ryn. And since he is possession of the block of frozen carbonite containing out good friend and rebel hero Dax Corbin, Gorath wants you to— I get it, Loquim. Of course I'll go. ―LQ-9M and Kast Around this time, Velgor had his armor damaged during a mission . Reflecting on the pain it caused him to repair, Velgor wondered if Kast's pain, including losing his hand, had made him stronger. Velgor suspected it had not and considered Kast weak, believing the fledgling Keeper had not accomplished anything without the help of his friends, who Velgor believed he allowed to survive. No longer wishing to turn Kast he resolved to kill him, and began his path to do so by searching for Dax Corbin.
In killing Kast Velgor believed he would kill Zeth Malkor's last hope and prove those who thought Kast would be his end wrong.
While tracking Corbin, Velgor's partner, a Shade Covenant assassin named Ochi , figured out that Velgor planned to lure Kast to him with Corbin.
While practicing his flux blade and Flux skills by fighting training remotes, during which he recalled Vorn's training, Kast was interrupted by LQ-9M, insisting he did not mind the distraction. LQ-9M said that DR-7X wanted to speak with him about something related to Tempes, where he found his new flux blade. Before DR-7X could say anything, Gorath arrived and revealed, via LQ-9M translating, that Kael Ryn had been spotted on Nar Shaddaa and urged Kast to come with him to rescue Corbin from him. Gorath shot down Kast's suggestion that they bring Drev Kass, saying he was not to be trusted and that Corbin would not want to see him, but Kast agreed. Kast, LQ-9M, DR-7X, and Gorath then took the Shadow Hawk to Nar Shaddaa, though R2 complained to LQ-9M on the way that he had not been able to speak to Kast. Kast was annoyed that Venn had to stay behind to coordinate Operation Starlight and urged Gorath to ensure the mission would be quick, recalling how awful he found the planet and what had happened with Grakkus the Hutt.
On the planet, Kast watched as Gorath met with his contact Sagwa , LQ-9M explaining that he and Gorath used to be old friends and translating that Sagwa believed a new victor in the Garkata Fighting Arena was Kael Ryn. Looking at a hologram of the winner, Kast also thought they looked like Boba but noticed it was labeled "Jango." Kast spoke with a Pa'lowick attendant at the Arena to get information on "Jango" but was annoyed by both the Pa'lowick 's refusal to answer questions without payment and LQ-9M spouting the mottos of Nar Shaddaa. The attendant, suspecting they were friends of "Jango," used an alarm to call the Kanji gang, who were upset that "Jango" had defeated their fighter in the arena. Using his flux blade to fight back against the thugs, who attacked despite Kast's warnings, Kast and the others fled, though Gorath was reluctant to because they had not found much information. Following Sagwa through the streets before stealing and fleeing on a speeder, they encountered many more groups of Kanji that Kast fended off with the Synth Flux and his flux blade until DR-7X picked them up in the Shadow Hawk .
